import { assertAllowedFields, parseObjectBody, parseRequiredString } from '../request-parsing';
import {
  attributeSale,
  cancelAttribution,
  createRuleScope,
  generateVersionedCommission,
  listRuleScopes,
  reviseRuleScope,
  type RuleVersionInput,
} from '../commission-rule-versions';

const FORBIDDEN = ['agencyId', 'tenantId', 'id', 'seq', 'createdAt'] as const;
const VERSION_FIELDS = ['effectiveFrom', 'mode', 'calculationMetric', 'value', 'applyOn', 'excessThreshold', 'progressive', 'tiers', 'reason'] as const;

function num(value: unknown, field: string): number {
  if (typeof value !== 'number' || !Number.isFinite(value) || value < 0) throw new ValidationError(`Field "${field}" must be a non-negative number`);
  return value;
}

function parseVersion(value: unknown): RuleVersionInput {
  const record = parseObjectBody(value);
  assertAllowedFields(record, FORBIDDEN, VERSION_FIELDS);
  const mode = record.mode;
  if (mode !== 'PERCENTAGE' && mode !== 'FIXED' && mode !== 'NONE') throw new ValidationError('Field "mode" must be PERCENTAGE, FIXED or NONE');
  const metric = record.calculationMetric ?? 'GROSS';
  if (metric !== 'GROSS' && metric !== 'MARGIN') throw new ValidationError('Field "calculationMetric" must be GROSS or MARGIN');
  const applyOn = record.applyOn ?? 'TOTAL';
  if (applyOn !== 'TOTAL' && applyOn !== 'EXCESS') throw new ValidationError('Field "applyOn" must be TOTAL or EXCESS');
  const out: RuleVersionInput = {
    effectiveFrom: parseRequiredString(record.effectiveFrom, 'effectiveFrom'),
    mode,
    calculationMetric: metric,
    applyOn,
    progressive: record.progressive === true,
  };
  if (record.progressive !== undefined && typeof record.progressive !== 'boolean') throw new ValidationError('Field "progressive" must be a boolean');
  if (record.value !== undefined && record.value !== null) out.value = num(record.value, 'value');
  if (record.excessThreshold !== undefined && record.excessThreshold !== null) out.excessThreshold = num(record.excessThreshold, 'excessThreshold');
  if (record.reason !== undefined) out.reason = parseRequiredString(record.reason, 'reason').slice(0, 500);
  if (record.tiers !== undefined) {
    if (!Array.isArray(record.tiers) || record.tiers.length > 20) throw new ValidationError('Field "tiers" must be an array of at most 20 items');
    out.tiers = record.tiers.map((t: unknown) => {
      const tier = parseObjectBody(t);
      assertAllowedFields(tier, FORBIDDEN, ['fromAmount', 'toAmount', 'rate']);
      return { fromAmount: num(tier.fromAmount, 'fromAmount'), toAmount: tier.toAmount === null || tier.toAmount === undefined ? null : num(tier.toAmount, 'toAmount'), rate: num(tier.rate, 'rate') };
    });
  }
  return out;
}

function optionalId(value: unknown, field: string): string | null {
  if (value === undefined || value === null) return null;
  return parseRequiredString(value, field);
}

export function registerCommissionRuleVersionsRoutes(
  app: FastifyInstance,
  options: { database: DatabaseRuntime; protectedHooks: preHandlerHookHandler[] },
): void {
  const { database, protectedHooks } = options;

  app.get('/commission-rule-scopes', { preHandler: protectedHooks }, async () => {
    requireRole(UserRole.MANAGER);
    return { scopes: await listRuleScopes(database) };
  });

  app.post('/commission-rule-scopes', { preHandler: protectedHooks }, async (request, reply) => {
    requireRole(UserRole.ADMIN);
    const body = parseObjectBody(request.body);
    assertAllowedFields(body, FORBIDDEN, ['employeeId', 'categoryId', 'version']);
    const created = await createRuleScope(database, {
      employeeId: optionalId(body.employeeId, 'employeeId'),
      categoryId: optionalId(body.categoryId, 'categoryId'),
      version: parseVersion(body.version),
    });
    reply.code(201);
    return created;
  });

  app.post<{ Params: { id: string } }>('/commission-rule-scopes/:id/versions', { preHandler: protectedHooks }, async (request, reply) => {
    requireRole(UserRole.ADMIN);
    const created = await reviseRuleScope(database, request.params.id, parseVersion(request.body));
    reply.code(201);
    return created;
  });

  app.post<{ Params: { saleId: string } }>('/sales/:saleId/commission-attributions', { preHandler: protectedHooks }, async (request, reply) => {
    requireRole(UserRole.MANAGER);
    const body = parseObjectBody(request.body);
    assertAllowedFields(body, FORBIDDEN, ['employeeId', 'categoryId', 'recognitionDate']);
    const created = await attributeSale(database, {
      saleId: request.params.saleId,
      employeeId: parseRequiredString(body.employeeId, 'employeeId'),
      categoryId: optionalId(body.categoryId, 'categoryId'),
      ...(body.recognitionDate !== undefined ? { recognitionDate: parseRequiredString(body.recognitionDate, 'recognitionDate') } : {}),
    });
    reply.code(201);
    return created;
  });

  app.post<{ Params: { id: string } }>('/commission-attributions/:id/cancel', { preHandler: protectedHooks }, async (request) => {
    requireRole(UserRole.MANAGER);
    await cancelAttribution(database, request.params.id);
    return { ok: true };
  });

  app.post<{ Params: { saleId: string } }>('/sales/:saleId/commission-entries/versioned', { preHandler: protectedHooks }, async (request, reply) => {
    requireRole(UserRole.MANAGER);
    const body = parseObjectBody(request.body);
    assertAllowedFields(body, FORBIDDEN, ['employeeId']);
    const generated = await generateVersionedCommission(database, {
      saleId: request.params.saleId,
      employeeId: parseRequiredString(body.employeeId, 'employeeId'),
    });
    reply.code(201);
    return generated;
  });
}

function parseTiers(text: string): Array<{ fromAmount: number; toAmount: number | null; rate: number }> {
  return text
    .split('\n')
    .map((line) => line.trim())
    .filter((line) => line !== '')
    .map((line) => {
      const [from, to, rate] = line.split(';').map((part) => part.trim());
      const fromAmount = Number(from);
      const toAmount = to === undefined || to === '' ? null : Number(to);
      const parsedRate = Number(rate);
      if (!Number.isFinite(fromAmount) || (toAmount !== null && !Number.isFinite(toAmount)) || !Number.isFinite(parsedRate)) {
        throw new Error(`Faixa invǭlida: "${line}". Use de;atǸ;taxa (deixe "atǸ" vazio na ǧltima faixa).`);
      }
      return { fromAmount, toAmount, rate: parsedRate };
    });
}

function describeVersion(v: CommissionRuleScope['versions'][number], all: CommissionRuleScope['versions']): string {
  const rule = v.progressive ? `progressiva (${v.tiers.length} faixas)` : v.mode === 'PERCENTAGE' ? `${v.value ?? 0}%` : v.mode === 'FIXED' ? `fixo ${v.value ?? 0}` : 'sem regra';
  const winner = all.find((x) => x.id === v.supersededByVersionId);
  const state = v.isEffective ? 'em vigor' : `substitu��da pela v${winner?.versionNumber ?? '?'}`;
  return `${rule} �� ${v.calculationMetric === 'MARGIN' ? 'margem' : 'bruto'} �� ${state}`;
}

export function CommissionRulesPage() {
  const [scopes, setScopes] = useState<CommissionRuleScope[] | null>(null);
  const [employees, setEmployees] = useState<Employee[]>([]);
  const [categories, setCategories] = useState<SaleCategory[]>([]);
  const [error, setError] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const [employeeId, setEmployeeId] = useState('');
  const [categoryId, setCategoryId] = useState('');
  const [effectiveFrom, setEffectiveFrom] = useState('');
  const [metric, setMetric] = useState<'GROSS' | 'MARGIN'>('GROSS');
  const [percent, setPercent] = useState('');
  const [progressive, setProgressive] = useState(false);
  const [tiersText, setTiersText] = useState('');

  const load = useCallback(() => {
    Promise.all([listCommissionRuleScopes(), listEmployees(), listSaleCategories()])
      .then(([s, e, c]) => {
        setScopes(s);
        setEmployees(e);
        setCategories(c);
      })
      .catch((err: unknown) => setError(err instanceof ApiError ? err.message : 'Nǜo foi poss��vel carregar as regras.'));
  }, []);

  useEffect(() => {
    load();
  }, [load]);

  async function submit(event: FormEvent) {
    event.preventDefault();
    setError(null);
    try {
      const version: CommissionRuleVersionInput = {
        effectiveFrom,
        mode: 'PERCENTAGE',
        calculationMetric: metric,
        ...(progressive ? { progressive: true, tiers: parseTiers(tiersText) } : { value: Number(percent) }),
      };
      await createCommissionRuleScope({ employeeId: employeeId || null, categoryId: categoryId || null, version });
      load();
    } catch (err) {
      setError(err instanceof Error ? err.message : 'Nǜo foi poss��vel criar a regra.');
    }
  }

  if (scopes === null && error === null) return <LoadingState label="Carregando regras�?�" />;
  const nameOf = (id: string | null, list: Array<{ id: string; name: string }>, fallback: string) => (id ? list.find((i) => i.id === id)?.name ?? id : fallback);

  return (
    <div className="space-y-6">
      <PageHeader
        title="Regras de comissǜo"
        description="Regras por funcionǭrio e categoria, com revis��es que nunca apagam a anterior e faixas progressivas."
        breadcrumbs={[{ label: 'Pessoal' }, { label: 'Regras de comissǜo' }]}
      />
      {error !== null && <p role="alert" className="text-sm text-red-600">{error}</p>}
      <Card>
        <CardHeader><CardTitle>Nova regra</CardTitle></CardHeader>
        <CardContent>
          <form onSubmit={(event) => void submit(event)} className="grid gap-4 md:grid-cols-2">
            <div className="flex flex-col gap-1">
              <label htmlFor="cr-employee" className="text-xs font-medium text-slate-700">Funcionǭrio</label>
              <Select id="cr-employee" value={employeeId} onChange={(e) => setEmployeeId(e.target.value)}>
                <option value="">Padrǜo da agǦncia</option>
                {employees.map((e) => <option key={e.id} value={e.id}>{e.name}</option>)}
              </Select>
            </div>
            <div className="flex flex-col gap-1">
              <label htmlFor="cr-category" className="text-xs font-medium text-slate-700">Categoria</label>
              <Select id="cr-category" value={categoryId} onChange={(e) => setCategoryId(e.target.value)}>
                <option value="">Todas</option>
                {categories.map((c) => <option key={c.id} value={c.id}>{c.name}</option>)}
              </Select>
            </div>
            <div className="flex flex-col gap-1">
              <label htmlFor="cr-from" className="text-xs font-medium text-slate-700">VigǦncia a partir de</label>
              <Input id="cr-from" type="date" value={effectiveFrom} onChange={(e) => setEffectiveFrom(e.target.value)} />
            </div>
            <div className="flex flex-col gap-1">
              <label htmlFor="cr-metric" className="text-xs font-medium text-slate-700">Base</label>
              <Select id="cr-metric" value={metric} onChange={(e) => setMetric(e.target.value as 'GROSS' | 'MARGIN')}>
                <option value="GROSS">Valor bruto da venda</option>
                <option value="MARGIN">Margem</option>
              </Select>
            </div>
            <label className="flex items-center gap-2 text-sm md:col-span-2">
              <input type="checkbox" checked={progressive} onChange={(e) => setProgressive(e.target.checked)} aria-label="Progressiva por faixas" />
              Progressiva por faixas (acumulado do mǦs)
            </label>
            {progressive ? (
              <div className="flex flex-col gap-1 md:col-span-2">
                <label htmlFor="cr-tiers" className="text-xs font-medium text-slate-700">Faixas (de;atǸ;taxa por linha)</label>
                <textarea id="cr-tiers" className="min-h-24 rounded-md border border-slate-300 p-2 text-sm" value={tiersText} onChange={(e) => setTiersText(e.target.value)} />
              </div>
            ) : (
              <div className="flex flex-col gap-1">
                <label htmlFor="cr-percent" className="text-xs font-medium text-slate-700">Percentual (%)</label>
                <Input id="cr-percent" inputMode="decimal" value={percent} onChange={(e) => setPercent(e.target.value)} />
              </div>
            )}
            <div className="md:col-span-2"><Button type="submit">Criar regra</Button></div>
          </form>
        </CardContent>
      </Card>
      {(scopes ?? []).map((scope) => (
        <Card key={scope.id}>
          <CardHeader>
            <CardTitle>{nameOf(scope.employeeId, employees, 'Padrǜo da agǦncia')} �� {nameOf(scope.categoryId, categories, 'todas as categorias')}</CardTitle>
          </CardHeader>
          <CardContent>
            <ul className="space-y-1 text-sm">
              {scope.versions.map((v) => (
                <li key={v.id}>v{v.versionNumber} desde {v.effectiveFrom} �� {describeVersion(v, scope.versions)}</li>
              ))}
            </ul>
          </CardContent>
        </Card>
      ))}
    </div>
  );
}

329: async function accumulatedBefore(
330:   client: TenantTransactionClient,
331:   agencyId: string,
332:   employeeId: string,
333:   scopeCategoryId: string | null,
334:   metric: 'GROSS' | 'MARGIN',
335:   recognitionDate: string,
336:   seq: number,
337: ): Promise<number> {
338:   const month = recognitionDate.slice(0, 7);
339:   await client.query(`SELECT pg_advisory_xact_lock(hashtext($1))`, [`commission-accrual:${agencyId}:${employeeId}:${month}`]);
340:   const column = metric === 'MARGIN' ? 'a.margin_amount' : 'a.gross_amount';
341:   if (metric === 'MARGIN') {
342:     const missing = await client.query(
343:       `SELECT 1 FROM sale_commission_attributions a JOIN sales s ON s.agency_id = a.agency_id AND s.id = a.sale_id
344:         WHERE a.agency_id = $1 AND a.employee_id = $2 AND a.state = 'ACTIVE' AND s.status IN ('CONFIRMED', 'PAID')
345:           AND to_char(a.recognition_date, 'YYYY-MM') = $3 AND a.seq < $4 AND a.margin_amount IS NULL
346:           AND ($5::text IS NULL OR a.category_id = $5)`,
347:       [agencyId, employeeId, month, seq, scopeCategoryId],
348:     );
349:     if (missing.rows.length > 0) throw new ValidationError('An earlier attributed sale has no margin; a margin-based rule cannot accumulate');
350:   }
351:   const result = await client.query<{ total: string }>(
352:     `SELECT COALESCE(SUM(${column}), 0)::text AS total FROM sale_commission_attributions a
353:        JOIN sales s ON s.agency_id = a.agency_id AND s.id = a.sale_id
354:       WHERE a.agency_id = $1 AND a.employee_id = $2 AND a.state = 'ACTIVE' AND s.status IN ('CONFIRMED', 'PAID')
355:         AND to_char(a.recognition_date, 'YYYY-MM') = $3 AND a.seq < $4 AND ($5::text IS NULL OR a.category_id = $5)`,
356:     [agencyId, employeeId, month, seq, scopeCategoryId],
357:   );
358:   return Number(result.rows[0]!.total);
359: }

361: export async function generateVersionedCommission(
362:   database: DatabaseRuntime,
363:   input: { saleId: string; employeeId: string },
364: ): Promise<{ entryId: string; status: string; amount: number }> {
365:   const agencyId = getAgencyId();

367:     const attribution = await client.query<{

/**
 * Commission release policy and settlement balances (Onda E2: COM-03 + Frente 8) -- pure rules.
 *
 * Two axes (decision F2, dissolved): the payment method says WHETHER a sale needs the money received
 * (`requiresReceipt`, tri-state: null = never decided); the agency's financial profile says HOW that affects
 * goals and release. Missing configuration BLOCKS the feature and says what is missing: it is never
 * turned into "release everything now" (ON_SALE) by omission.
 *
 * `releasedAmount` follows the Lite formula (finance.ts loadCommissionRelease): amount x min(received/total, 1),
 * rounded half away from zero on cents. Entry status is derived from the SUM of what was really paid
 * (all channels), compared in cents.
 */

export type ReleaseEffect = 'META_E_LIBERACAO' | 'SOMENTE_LIBERACAO';

export type MissingConfig = 'PAYMENT_METHOD_NOT_SET' | 'PAYMENT_METHOD_RECEIPT_RULE_UNSET' | 'FINANCIAL_PROFILE_NOT_ACTIVE';

export type ReleasePolicy =
  | { status: 'RESOLVED'; requiresReceipt: false }
  | { status: 'RESOLVED'; requiresReceipt: true; effect: ReleaseEffect; profileId?: string }
  | { status: 'LEGACY_PRE_FEATURE' }
  | { status: 'BLOCKED'; missing: MissingConfig[] };

export function resolveReleasePolicy(input: {
  hasPaymentMethod: boolean;
  /** null/undefined = the method's rule was never configured. */
  requiresReceipt: boolean | null | undefined;
  profile: { effect: ReleaseEffect; profileId?: string } | null;
}): ReleasePolicy {
  const missing: MissingConfig[] = [];
  if (!input.hasPaymentMethod) missing.push('PAYMENT_METHOD_NOT_SET');
  else if (input.requiresReceipt === null || input.requiresReceipt === undefined) missing.push('PAYMENT_METHOD_RECEIPT_RULE_UNSET');
  // the profile governs sales that require receipt; an undecided or missing method keeps it relevant
  const profileNeeded = input.requiresReceipt !== false;
  if (profileNeeded && input.profile === null) missing.push('FINANCIAL_PROFILE_NOT_ACTIVE');
  if (missing.length > 0) return { status: 'BLOCKED', missing };
  if (input.requiresReceipt === false) return { status: 'RESOLVED', requiresReceipt: false };
  const profile = input.profile!;
  return {
    status: 'RESOLVED',
    requiresReceipt: true,
    effect: profile.effect,
    ...(profile.profileId !== undefined ? { profileId: profile.profileId } : {}),
  };
}

function roundCents(value: number): number {
  const cents = Number((Math.abs(value) * 100).toPrecision(15));
  return (Math.sign(value) * Math.round(cents)) / 100 || 0;
}

const toCents = (value: number): number => Math.round(Number((value * 100).toPrecision(15)));

/** Amount of the recognized commission that may be paid so far. */
export function releasedAmount(policy: ReleasePolicy, recognized: number, received: number, total: number): number {
  if (policy.status === 'BLOCKED') return 0;
  if (policy.status === 'LEGACY_PRE_FEATURE' || !policy.requiresReceipt) return recognized;
  if (!(total > 0)) return 0;
  const ratio = Math.min(Math.max(received, 0) / total, 1);
  return roundCents(recognized * ratio);
}

/** What can still be allocated now: released minus already paid, never negative. */
export function allocableAmount(released: number, paid: number): number {
  return Math.max(0, toCents(released) - toCents(paid)) / 100;
}

/**
 * Entry status from the SUM of real payments (any channel). A partial payment never marks the whole
 * entry PAID. Payable channel stays PAYABLE, payroll channel stays APPROVED until fully paid.
 */
export function entryStatusAfterPayments(input: {
  recognized: number;
  paid: number;
  channel: 'PAYABLE' | 'PAYROLL';
}): 'PAID' | 'PAYABLE' | 'APPROVED' {
  if (toCents(input.paid) >= toCents(input.recognized)) return 'PAID';
  return input.channel === 'PAYABLE' ? 'PAYABLE' : 'APPROVED';
}

export async function reactivateRevenueEntry(database: DatabaseRuntime, entryId: string, reason: string) {
  requireRole(UserRole.ADMIN);
  const agencyId = getAgencyId();
  if (typeof reason !== 'string' || !reason.trim() || reason.trim().length > 128) throw new ValidationError('A reason of 1 to 128 characters is required');
  return database.withTenantTransaction(async (client) => {
    const saleId = await lockEntrySale(client, agencyId, entryId);
    const eligible = await client.query<EntryRow>(
      `SELECT e.id, e.sale_item_id, e.status, e.entitlement_event, e.amount FROM agency_revenue_entries e
       JOIN sale_items i ON i.agency_id=e.agency_id AND i.id=e.sale_item_id AND i.sale_id=e.sale_id
       JOIN sales s ON s.agency_id=e.agency_id AND s.id=e.sale_id
       WHERE e.agency_id=$1 AND e.id=$2 AND i.status='ACTIVE' AND s.status <> 'CANCELLED' FOR UPDATE OF e`,
      [agencyId, entryId]);
    const row = eligible.rows[0];
    if (!row || row.status !== 'CANCELLED') throw new ConflictError('Only cancelled revenue of an active item and sale can be reactivated');
    const live = await client.query(`SELECT 1 FROM agency_revenue_entries
      WHERE agency_id=$1 AND sale_item_id=$2 AND status <> 'CANCELLED'`, [agencyId, row.sale_item_id]);
    if (live.rowCount) throw new ConflictError('The item already has live revenue');
    await client.query(`UPDATE agency_revenue_entries SET
      status=CASE WHEN rule_version_id IS NULL OR memory ? 'reason' THEN 'PENDING_RULE' ELSE 'FORECAST' END,
      cancelled_at=NULL, entitled_at=NULL, updated_at=now() WHERE agency_id=$1 AND id=$2`, [agencyId, entryId]);
    await recordAuditEvent(client, {
      eventType: AuditEventType.AGENCY_REVENUE_REACTIVATED, entityType: 'agency_revenue_entry', entityId: entryId,
      required: true,
      metadata: { reasonCode: reason.trim() },
    });
    await syncRevenueEntitlement(client, saleId);
    return { id: entryId };
  });
}

export async function receiveRevenueEntry(
  database: DatabaseRuntime,
  entryId: string,
  input: { amount: number; reference: string | null },
) {
  requireRole(UserRole.ADMIN);
  if (!Number.isFinite(input.amount) || input.amount < 0) throw new ValidationError('Field "amount" is invalid');
  return database.withTenantTransaction(async (client) => {
    const agencyId = getAgencyId();
    await lockEntrySale(client, agencyId, entryId);
    const entry = await client.query<EntryRow>(
      `SELECT id, sale_item_id, status, entitlement_event, amount FROM agency_revenue_entries
        WHERE agency_id = $1 AND id = $2 FOR UPDATE`,
      [getAgencyId(), entryId],
    );
    const row = entry.rows[0];
    if (!row) throw new NotFoundError('Revenue entry not found');
    if (row.status !== 'ENTITLED' && row.status !== 'AVAILABLE') {
      throw new ConflictError(`Cannot receive a ${row.status} entry (the right must exist first)`);
    }
    await setStatus(client, row.id, 'RECEIVED', AuditEventType.AGENCY_REVENUE_RECEIVED, {
      receivedAmount: input.amount,
      reference: input.reference,
    });
    return { id: row.id, expected: Number(row.amount), received: input.amount, difference: Math.round((Number(row.amount) - input.amount) * 100) / 100 };
  });
}

export async function cancelRevenueEntry(database: DatabaseRuntime, entryId: string) {
  requireRole(UserRole.ADMIN);
  return database.withTenantTransaction(async (client) => {
    const agencyId = getAgencyId();
    await lockEntrySale(client, agencyId, entryId);
    const entry = await client.query<EntryRow>(
      `SELECT id, sale_item_id, status, entitlement_event, amount FROM agency_revenue_entries
        WHERE agency_id = $1 AND id = $2 FOR UPDATE`,
      [getAgencyId(), entryId],
    );
    const row = entry.rows[0];
    if (!row) throw new NotFoundError('Revenue entry not found');
    if (row.status === 'RECEIVED') throw new ConflictError('A received entry cannot be cancelled');
    if (row.status === 'CANCELLED') return { id: row.id, status: 'CANCELLED' as const };
    await setStatus(client, row.id, 'CANCELLED', AuditEventType.AGENCY_REVENUE_CANCELLED);
    return { id: row.id, status: 'CANCELLED' as const };
  });
}

interface EntryView {
  id: string;
  sale_item_id: string;
  supplier_id: string | null;
  rule_version_id: string | null;
  status: EntryStatus;
  entitlement_event: EntitlementEvent | null;
  amount: number;
  base_amount: number;
  calc_version: number;
  memory: Record<string, unknown>;
  entitled_at: Date | null;
  received_at: Date | null;
  received_amount: number | null;
}

async function loadEntries(client: TenantTransactionClient, saleId: string) {
  const result = await client.query<EntryView>(
    `SELECT id, sale_item_id, supplier_id, rule_version_id, status, entitlement_event, amount::float8 AS amount,
            base_amount::float8 AS base_amount, calc_version, memory, entitled_at, received_at, received_amount::float8 AS received_amount
       FROM agency_revenue_entries WHERE agency_id = $1 AND sale_id = $2 ORDER BY created_at, id`,
    [getAgencyId(), saleId],
  );
  return result.rows;
}

/** Read model: revenue entries plus an analysis-only comparison with the employee commission (no netting). */
export async function getSaleRevenue(database: DatabaseRuntime, saleId: string) {
  requireRole(UserRole.MANAGER);
  return database.withTenantTransaction(async (client) => {
    const sale = await client.query(`SELECT 1 FROM sales WHERE agency_id = $1 AND id = $2`, [getAgencyId(), saleId]);
    if (sale.rowCount === 0) throw new NotFoundError('Sale not found');
    const entries = await loadEntries(client, saleId);
    const commission = await client.query<{ total: string }>(
      `SELECT COALESCE(SUM(amount), 0) AS total FROM commission_entries
        WHERE agency_id = $1 AND sale_id = $2 AND status <> 'CANCELLED'`,
      [getAgencyId(), saleId],
    );
    const sum = (statuses: string[], field: 'amount' | 'received_amount' = 'amount') =>
      Math.round(entries.filter((entry) => statuses.includes(entry.status as string)).reduce((total, entry) => total + Math.round(Number(entry[field] ?? 0) * 100), 0)) / 100;
    const projected = sum(['FORECAST', 'ENTITLED', 'AVAILABLE', 'RECEIVED']);
    const expectedReceived = sum(['RECEIVED']);
    const actualReceived = sum(['RECEIVED'], 'received_amount');
    const employeeCommission = Math.round(Number(commission.rows[0]?.total ?? 0) * 100) / 100;
    return {
      entries,
      totals: {
        forecast: sum(['FORECAST']),
        entitled: sum(['ENTITLED', 'AVAILABLE']),
        expected: expectedReceived,
        received: actualReceived,
        difference: Math.round((expectedReceived - actualReceived) * 100) / 100,
        pendingRule: entries.filter((entry) => entry.status === 'PENDING_RULE').length,
      },
      // Analysis only (D3): two independent obligations, never netted or linked by payment.
      analysis: {
        agencyRevenue: projected,
        employeeCommission,
        difference: Math.round((projected - employeeCommission) * 100) / 100,
      },
    };
  });
}
import type { FastifyInstance, preHandlerHookHandler } from 'fastify';
import { requireRole } from '../../../../packages/domain/tenant-context';
import { UserRole } from '../../../../packages/domain/types';
import {
  calculateRevenueForSale,
  cancelRevenueEntry,
  createRuleVersion,
  getSaleRevenue,
  listRuleVersions,
  parseRuleInput,
  receiveRevenueEntry,
  reactivateRevenueEntry,
} from '../agency-revenue';
import type { DatabaseRuntime } from '../database';
import { ValidationError } from '../errors';

export function registerAgencyRevenueRoutes(
  app: FastifyInstance,
  options: { database: DatabaseRuntime; protectedHooks: preHandlerHookHandler[] },
): void {
  const { database, protectedHooks } = options;

  app.get('/agency-revenue/rules', { preHandler: protectedHooks }, async () => {
    requireRole(UserRole.MANAGER);
    return { rules: await listRuleVersions(database) };
  });

  app.post('/agency-revenue/rules', { preHandler: protectedHooks }, async (request, reply) => {
    requireRole(UserRole.ADMIN);
    const created = await createRuleVersion(database, parseRuleInput(request.body));
    reply.code(201);
    return { rule: created };
  });

  app.get<{ Params: { id: string } }>('/sales/:id/agency-revenue', { preHandler: protectedHooks }, async (request) => {
    requireRole(UserRole.MANAGER);
    return getSaleRevenue(database, request.params.id);
  });

  app.post<{ Params: { id: string } }>('/sales/:id/agency-revenue/calculate', { preHandler: protectedHooks }, async (request) => {
    requireRole(UserRole.MANAGER);
    return calculateRevenueForSale(database, request.params.id);
  });

  app.post<{ Params: { id: string } }>('/agency-revenue/entries/:id/receive', { preHandler: protectedHooks }, async (request) => {
    requireRole(UserRole.ADMIN);
    const body = request.body;
    if (!body || typeof body !== 'object' || Array.isArray(body)) throw new ValidationError('Request body must be an object');
    const record = body as Record<string, unknown>;
    if (Object.keys(record).some((key) => !['amount', 'reference'].includes(key))) throw new ValidationError('Unknown field in request body');
    if (typeof record.amount !== 'number') throw new ValidationError('Field "amount" must be a number');
    if (record.reference !== undefined && (typeof record.reference !== 'string' || record.reference.length > 200)) {
      throw new ValidationError('Field "reference" is invalid');
    }
    return receiveRevenueEntry(database, request.params.id, {
      amount: record.amount,
      reference: typeof record.reference === 'string' ? record.reference : null,
    });
  });

  app.post<{ Params: { id: string } }>('/agency-revenue/entries/:id/cancel', { preHandler: protectedHooks }, async (request) => {
    requireRole(UserRole.ADMIN);
    return cancelRevenueEntry(database, request.params.id);
  });
  app.post<{ Params: { id: string } }>('/agency-revenue/entries/:id/reactivate', { preHandler: protectedHooks }, async (request) => {
    requireRole(UserRole.ADMIN);
    const body = request.body;
    if (!body || typeof body !== 'object' || Array.isArray(body)) throw new ValidationError('Request body must be an object');
    const record = body as Record<string, unknown>;
    if (Object.keys(record).some((key) => key !== 'reason') || typeof record.reason !== 'string') {
      throw new ValidationError('A reason is required');
    }
    return reactivateRevenueEntry(database, request.params.id, record.reason);
  });
}
import { useEffect, useState } from 'react';
import { PageHeader } from '../components/layout/PageHeader';
import { Card, CardContent, CardHeader, CardTitle } from '../components/ui/card';
import { api } from '../lib/api';

interface Rule {
  id: string;
  mode: string;
  base_selector: string | null;
  value: number;
  effective_from: string;
  version_number: number;
  entitlement_event: string;
  collection_model: string;
}

interface Entry {
  id: string;
  status: string;
  amount: number;
  base_amount: number;
  received_amount: number | null;
  memory: Record<string, unknown>;
}

interface SaleRevenue {
  entries: Entry[];
  totals: { forecast: number; entitled: number; received: number; pendingRule: number };
  analysis: { agencyRevenue: number; employeeCommission: number; difference: number };
}

const STATUS_LABEL: Record<string, string> = {
  PENDING_RULE: 'Pendente de regra',
  FORECAST: 'Prevista',
  ENTITLED: 'Direito constitu��do',
  AVAILABLE: 'Dispon��vel',
  RECEIVED: 'Recebida do fornecedor',
  CANCELLED: 'Cancelada',
};

const money = (value: number) =>
  new Intl.NumberFormat('pt-BR', { style: 'currency', currency: 'BRL' }).format(value);

/**
 * Agency revenue (ADR-007): what the supplier owes the AGENCY. Kept apart from
 * the employee commission; the figures below are analysis only (never netted).
 * The server calculates everything -- this page only asks and displays.
 */
export function AgencyRevenuePage() {
  const [rules, setRules] = useState<Rule[]>([]);
  const [saleId, setSaleId] = useState('');
  const [revenue, setRevenue] = useState<SaleRevenue | null>(null);
  const [error, setError] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const [busy, setBusy] = useState(false);

  useEffect(() => {
    api
      .get<{ rules: Rule[] }>('/agency-revenue/rules')
      .then(({ data }) => setRules(data.rules))
      .catch(() => setError('Nǜo foi poss��vel carregar as regras de receita.'));
  }, []);

  async function load() {
    setBusy(true);
    setError(null);
    try {
      const { data } = await api.get<SaleRevenue>(`/sales/${encodeURIComponent(saleId.trim())}/agency-revenue`);
      setRevenue(data);
    } catch (err) {
      setError(err instanceof Error ? err.message : 'Nǜo foi poss��vel consultar a venda.');
    } finally {
      setBusy(false);
    }
  }

  async function calculate() {
    setBusy(true);
    setError(null);
    try {
      await api.post(`/sales/${encodeURIComponent(saleId.trim())}/agency-revenue/calculate`);
      const { data } = await api.get<SaleRevenue>(`/sales/${encodeURIComponent(saleId.trim())}/agency-revenue`);
      setRevenue(data);
    } catch (err) {
      setError(err instanceof Error ? err.message : 'Nǜo foi poss��vel calcular a receita.');
    } finally {
      setBusy(false);
    }
  }

  return (
    <div className="space-y-6">
      <PageHeader title="Receita da agǦncia" />
      <Card>
        <CardHeader>
          <CardTitle>Regras</CardTitle>
        </CardHeader>
        <CardContent>
          {rules.length === 0 ? (
            <p className="text-sm text-slate-500">Nenhuma regra cadastrada.</p>
          ) : (
            <ul className="space-y-1 text-sm text-slate-700">
              {rules.map((rule) => (
                <li key={rule.id}>
                  v{rule.version_number} �� {rule.mode}
                  {rule.base_selector ? ` �� ${rule.base_selector}` : ''} �� {rule.value}
                  {rule.mode === 'PERCENTAGE' ? '%' : ''} �� a partir de {rule.effective_from} ��{' '}
                  {rule.collection_model === 'AGENCY_COLLECTS' ? 'agǦncia recebe do cliente' : 'fornecedor recebe do cliente'}
                </li>
              ))}
            </ul>
          )}
        </CardContent>
      </Card>

      <Card>
        <CardHeader>
          <CardTitle>Venda</CardTitle>
        </CardHeader>
        <CardContent className="space-y-3">
          <div className="flex flex-wrap items-end gap-3">
            <div>
              <label htmlFor="revenue-sale-id" className="block text-xs text-slate-500">
                ID da venda
              </label>
              <input
                id="revenue-sale-id"
                value={saleId}
                onChange={(event) => setSaleId(event.target.value)}
                className="mt-1 rounded-md border border-slate-300 px-3 py-2 text-sm"
              />
            </div>
            <button
              type="button"
              disabled={busy || saleId.trim() === ''}
              onClick={() => void load()}
              className="rounded-md border border-slate-300 px-3 py-2 text-sm"
            >
              Consultar
            </button>
            <button
              type="button"
              disabled={busy || saleId.trim() === ''}
              onClick={() => void calculate()}
              className="rounded-md border border-slate-300 px-3 py-2 text-sm"
            >
              Calcular receita
            </button>
          </div>
          {error && (
            <p role="alert" className="text-sm text-red-700">
              {error}
            </p>
          )}
          {revenue && (
            <div className="space-y-3 text-sm text-slate-700">
              <ul className="space-y-1">
                {revenue.entries.map((entry) => (
                  <li key={entry.id}>
                    {STATUS_LABEL[entry.status] ?? entry.status} �� Esperado: {money(entry.amount)}
                {entry.status === 'RECEIVED' && (
                  <span>
                    {' �� '}Recebido: {money(entry.received_amount ?? 0)}
                    {' �� '}Diferen��a esperado versus recebido: {money(entry.amount - (entry.received_amount ?? 0))}
                  </span>
                )}
                    {entry.base_amount > 0 ? ` (base ${money(entry.base_amount)})` : ''}
                  </li>
                ))}
              </ul>
              <div className="rounded-md bg-slate-50 p-3">
                <p className="text-xs font-medium text-slate-500">Anǭlise (valores independentes, sem abatimento)</p>
                <p>Receita da agǦncia: {money(revenue.analysis.agencyRevenue)}</p>
                <p>Comissǜo do funcionǭrio: {money(revenue.analysis.employeeCommission)}</p>
                <p>Diferen��a receita versus comissǜo: {money(revenue.analysis.difference)}</p>
              </div>
            </div>
          )}
        </CardContent>
      </Card>
    </div>
  );
}

.claude/ESPECIFICACAO-AJUSTES-GADOTTI-2026-10-23.md:386:- `GET /sales/:id/balance` → `{ total, received, remaining, receivables:[{kind,amount,paid,due,status}], next_due, last_payment_at, settled_at, down_payment_pending, balance_deadline, late }`.
.claude/ESPECIFICACAO-AJUSTES-GADOTTI-2026-10-23.md:388:- `settled_at` gravado quando `remaining` chega a 0; **estorno** que reabre saldo limpa `settled_at` e **reverte efeitos dependentes** (direito de receita ainda não recebido volta a `FORECAST`; já recebido → bloqueia estorno com 409, igual a F3).
.claude/ESPECIFICACAO-AJUSTES-GADOTTI-2026-10-23.md:403:**Requisito (fonte, linhas 9–15):** a agência **revende**; recebe **% sobre o total**, **% sobre parte** (taxas, tarifas…), **fixo por passageiro** (ônibus intermunicipal), **só taxas** (cruzeiro), **vários fornecedores com % distintos** (aéreo/pacotes); produto atrelado a fornecedor; fornecedor com configuração de comissão; direito, liberação e recebimento efetivo.
.claude/ESPECIFICACAO-AJUSTES-GADOTTI-2026-10-23.md:430:- **Cálculo por item e por fornecedor** (aéreo com vários): `receita_item = round(base_item × taxa/100)` (half-up decimal-safe, D4); `receita_venda = Σ receita_item`; cada item é uma linha em `agency_revenue_entries` com **snapshot completo** (versão, base, taxa, memória de cálculo, `calc_version`). Itens sem fornecedor/regra aplicável → entrada `PENDING_RULE` (valor 0, **fora** de relatórios de receita reconhecida, igual padrão de comissão).
.claude/ESPECIFICACAO-AJUSTES-GADOTTI-2026-10-23.md:431:- **Entradas** `agency_revenue_entries(id, sale_id, sale_item_id?, supplier_id, rule_version_id, base_amount, rate_or_fixed, amount, status, entitled_at, batch_id?, received_amount, snapshot, legacy_ref)`; **estados**: `FORECAST` → `ENTITLED` → `AVAILABLE` (em lote/janela) → `RECEIVED` | `CANCELLED`. Transições só no servidor, uma função única de domínio, com auditoria.
.claude/ESPECIFICACAO-AJUSTES-GADOTTI-2026-10-23.md:433:- **Arrecadação (D1)**: parâmetro `collection_model` por fornecedor/produto: `AGENCY_COLLECTS` (padrão proposto = comportamento atual do Lite: recebíveis do total + custos/pagáveis) ou `SUPPLIER_COLLECTS` (cliente paga ao fornecedor; agência só rastreia pagamento para fins de **direito** e recebe só a comissão). Sem essa escolha **não calcular fluxo de caixa**.

import { assertEntryReleasableForPayable } from './commission-release-service';
import {
  CommissionCalculationType,
  CommissionEntryStatus,
  type CommissionEntry,
} from '../../../packages/domain/types';
import { getAgencyId } from '../../../packages/domain/tenant-context';
import type { DatabaseRuntime } from './database';
import { ConflictError, NotFoundError, ValidationError } from './errors';
import { getSaleMargin } from './financial';

interface CommissionEntryRow {
  id: string;
  agency_id: string;
  employee_id: string;
  sale_id: string;
  trip_id: string | null;
  commission_plan_id: string | null;
  commission_rule_id: string | null;
  product_type: CommissionEntry['productType'] | null;
  source_item_id: string | null;
  source_item_type: string | null;
  quantity: string | null;
  calculation_base: string;
  rate: string | null;
  amount: string;
  status: CommissionEntryStatus;
  approved_at: string | null;
  approved_by: string | null;
  paid_at: string | null;
  notes: string | null;
  created_at: string;
  updated_at: string;
}

const COMMISSION_ENTRY_COLUMNS = `id, agency_id, employee_id, sale_id, trip_id, commission_plan_id,
  commission_rule_id, product_type, source_item_id, source_item_type, quantity,
  calculation_base, rate, amount, status, approved_at, approved_by, paid_at, notes,
  created_at, updated_at`;

export interface GenerateCommissionInput {
  saleId: string;
  employeeId: string;
  commissionPlanId?: string | undefined;
  notes?: string | undefined;
}

export interface CommissionListFilters {
  employeeId?: string | undefined;
  saleId?: string | undefined;
  status?: string | undefined;
}

function toCommissionEntry(row: CommissionEntryRow): CommissionEntry {
  return {
    id: row.id,
    agencyId: row.agency_id,
    employeeId: row.employee_id,
    saleId: row.sale_id,
    tripId: row.trip_id ?? undefined,
    commissionPlanId: row.commission_plan_id ?? undefined,
    commissionRuleId: row.commission_rule_id ?? undefined,
    productType: row.product_type ?? undefined,
    sourceItemId: row.source_item_id ?? undefined,
    sourceItemType: row.source_item_type ?? undefined,
    quantity: row.quantity !== null ? Number(row.quantity) : undefined,
    calculationBase: Number(row.calculation_base),
    rate: row.rate !== null ? Number(row.rate) : undefined,
    amount: Number(row.amount),
    status: row.status,
    approvedAt: row.approved_at !== null ? new Date(row.approved_at) : undefined,
    approvedBy: row.approved_by ?? undefined,
    paidAt: row.paid_at !== null ? new Date(row.paid_at) : undefined,
    notes: row.notes ?? undefined,
    createdAt: new Date(row.created_at),
    updatedAt: new Date(row.updated_at),
  };
}

export async function listCommissionEntries(
  database: DatabaseRuntime,
  filters: CommissionListFilters = {},
): Promise<CommissionEntry[]> {
  const agencyId = getAgencyId();
  const conditions = ['agency_id = $1'];
  const values: unknown[] = [agencyId];

  if (filters.employeeId) {
    values.push(filters.employeeId);
    conditions.push(`employee_id = $${values.length}`);
  }
  if (filters.saleId) {
    values.push(filters.saleId);
    conditions.push(`sale_id = $${values.length}`);
  }
  if (filters.status) {
    values.push(filters.status);
    conditions.push(`status = $${values.length}`);
  }

  const rows = await database.withTenantTransaction(async (client) => {
    const result = await client.query<CommissionEntryRow>(
      `SELECT ${COMMISSION_ENTRY_COLUMNS} FROM commission_entries
       WHERE ${conditions.join(' AND ')} ORDER BY created_at DESC`,
      values,
    );
    return result.rows;
  });

  return rows.map(toCommissionEntry);
}

export async function getCommissionEntryById(
  database: DatabaseRuntime,
  id: string,
): Promise<CommissionEntry | null> {
  const agencyId = getAgencyId();
  const row = await database.withTenantTransaction(async (client) => {
    const result = await client.query<CommissionEntryRow>(
      `SELECT ${COMMISSION_ENTRY_COLUMNS} FROM commission_entries WHERE agency_id = $1 AND id = $2`,
      [agencyId, id],
    );
    return result.rows[0] ?? null;
  });
  return row ? toCommissionEntry(row) : null;
}

918:   database: DatabaseRuntime,
919:   revenueId: string,
920:   partialAmount?: number,
921: ): Promise<Revenue> {
922:   const agencyId = getAgencyId();
923:   if (partialAmount !== undefined) {
924:     assertPositiveMoney(partialAmount, 'partialAmount');
925:   }
926: 
927:   return database.withTenantTransaction(async (client) => {
928:     const existing = await client.query<RevenueRow>(
929:       `SELECT ${REVENUE_COLUMNS} FROM revenues WHERE agency_id = $1 AND id = $2 FOR UPDATE`,
930:       [agencyId, revenueId],
931:     );
932:     const row = existing.rows[0];
933:     if (!row) throw new NotFoundError('Revenue not found');
934: 
935:     // eslint-disable-next-line @typescript-eslint/no-unsafe-enum-comparison
936:     if (row.status === 'PAID') {
937:       throw new ValidationError('Cannot update a paid revenue');
938:     }
939: 
940:     const newStatus = partialAmount === undefined ? 'PAID' : 'PARTIALLY_PAID';
941: 
942:     const result = await client.query<RevenueRow>(
943:       `UPDATE revenues SET
944:          status = $2::"RevenueStatus",
945:          receipt_date = COALESCE(receipt_date, CASE WHEN $2::"RevenueStatus" = 'PAID' THEN now() ELSE NULL END),
946:          updated_at = now()
947:        WHERE agency_id = $1 AND id = $3
948:        RETURNING ${REVENUE_COLUMNS}`,
949:       [agencyId, newStatus, revenueId],
950:     );
951:

953:     if (!updated) throw new NotFoundError('Revenue not found');
954:     return toRevenue(updated);
955:   });

993:     let paramIndex = 2;

996:       query += ` AND status = $${paramIndex}`;
997:       params.push(filters.status);
998:       paramIndex++;
999:     }
1000:     if (filters?.supplierId) {
1001:       query += ` AND supplier_id = $${paramIndex}`;
1002:       params.push(filters.supplierId);
1003:       paramIndex++;

1012:       params.push(filters.periodFrom);
1013:       paramIndex++;
1014:     }

1016:       query += ` AND due_date <= $${paramIndex}`;

1020:

2953:   column: string,

2966:   );
2967:   return Number(result.rows[0]?.sum ?? 0);

-- ============================================================
-- 100_commission_channel_protection.sql
-- Comiss��es, Fase 0 da paridade Full/Lite: pagamento ǧnico por comissǜo.
--   * um payable vivo (nǜo cancelado) por entrada de comissǜo;
--   * uma entrada pertence a, no mǭximo, UMA folha (payroll_entries): a folha
--     "reserva" a entrada; enquanto reservada ela nǜo pode ter payable avulso,
--     e uma entrada com payable avulso nǜo entra na folha (canal ǧnico).
-- Aditiva. Nǜo altera nem apaga dados: se jǭ existirem payables vivos
-- duplicados para a mesma entrada, a migration ABORTA com erro claro e o
-- problema deve ser resolvido pelo responsǭvel antes de reaplicar.
-- Entradas/folhas existentes NǟO recebem v��nculo retroativo (legado em
-- conflito Ǹ s�� reportado: ver listCommissionChannelConflicts).
-- ============================================================

DO $$
BEGIN
  IF EXISTS (
    SELECT 1 FROM payables
     WHERE commission_entry_id IS NOT NULL AND status <> 'CANCELLED'
     GROUP BY agency_id, commission_entry_id HAVING count(*) > 1
  ) THEN
    RAISE EXCEPTION 'Duplicate live payables per commission entry exist; resolve them before applying 100';
  END IF;
END $$;

CREATE UNIQUE INDEX payables_agency_commission_entry_live_key
  ON payables (agency_id, commission_entry_id)
  WHERE commission_entry_id IS NOT NULL AND status <> 'CANCELLED';

ALTER TABLE commission_entries
  ADD COLUMN payroll_entry_id TEXT;

ALTER TABLE commission_entries
  ADD CONSTRAINT commission_entries_payroll_entry_tenant_fk
    FOREIGN KEY (agency_id, payroll_entry_id) REFERENCES payroll_entries (agency_id, id)
    ON DELETE RESTRICT ON UPDATE CASCADE;

CREATE INDEX commission_entries_agency_payroll_entry_idx
  ON commission_entries (agency_id, payroll_entry_id)
  WHERE payroll_entry_id IS NOT NULL;

async function loadRevenueRows(database: DatabaseRuntime, filters: ProjectionFilters): Promise<RevenueRow[]> {
  const params: unknown[] = [getAgencyId()];
  const where = saleWhere(filters, params);
  const extra: string[] = [];
  if (filters.supplierId) {
    params.push(filters.supplierId);
    extra.push(`e.supplier_id = $${params.length}`);
  }
  if (filters.productId) {
    params.push(filters.productId);
    extra.push(`i.product_id = $${params.length}`);
  }
  const rows = await database.withTenantTransaction(async (client) => {
    const result = await client.query<{
      id: string; supplier_id: string | null; status: string; amount: string; received_amount: string | null;
      entitled_on: string | null; expected_on: string | null;
    }>(
      `SELECT e.id, e.supplier_id, e.status, e.amount::text AS amount, e.received_amount::text AS received_amount,
              to_char(e.entitled_at AT TIME ZONE 'America/Sao_Paulo', 'YYYY-MM-DD') AS entitled_on,
              CASE WHEN e.status = 'FORECAST' AND e.entitlement_event <> 'ON_SALE_CONFIRMATION' THEN
                (SELECT MAX(COALESCE(r.due_date::text, to_char(r.due_at AT TIME ZONE 'America/Sao_Paulo', 'YYYY-MM-DD')))
                   FROM receivables r WHERE r.agency_id = e.agency_id AND r.sale_id = e.sale_id AND r.status::text <> 'CANCELLED')
              END AS expected_on
         FROM agency_revenue_entries e
         JOIN sales s ON s.agency_id = e.agency_id AND s.id = e.sale_id
         JOIN sale_items i ON i.agency_id = e.agency_id AND i.id = e.sale_item_id
        WHERE ${where}${extra.length ? ` AND ${extra.join(' AND ')}` : ''} AND e.status <> 'CANCELLED'
        ORDER BY e.created_at, e.id
        LIMIT ${PROJECTION_ROW_LIMIT + 1}`,
      params,
    );
    return result.rows;
  });
  if (rows.length > PROJECTION_ROW_LIMIT) throw new ValidationError('Too many rows for this report; narrow the filters');
  return rows.map((row) => ({
    entryId: row.id,
    supplierId: row.supplier_id,
    status: row.status,
    amount: Number(row.amount),
    receivedAmount: row.received_amount === null ? null : Number(row.received_amount),
    entitledOn: row.entitled_on,
    expectedOn: row.expected_on,
  }));
}

export async function getRevenueForecast(database: DatabaseRuntime, filters: ProjectionFilters) {
  requireRole(UserRole.MANAGER);
  return projectRevenue(await loadRevenueRows(database, filters));
}

export async function getSupplierBalances(database: DatabaseRuntime, filters: ProjectionFilters) {
  requireRole(UserRole.MANAGER);
  return supplierBalances(await loadRevenueRows(database, filters));
}

/** Employee commission: forecast x released by customer receipts x paid (the revenue is never mixed in). */
/** One lookup per entry (the release state is computed per entry), so this report has a lower ceiling. */
export const COMMISSION_PROJECTION_LIMIT = 1000;

export async function getCommissionsProjection(database: DatabaseRuntime, filters: ProjectionFilters) {
  requireRole(UserRole.MANAGER);
  const restrictionContext = getTenantContext();
  // Same restriction as the source employee-commission report.
  await database.withTenantTransaction((client) =>
    assertNotRestricted(client, restrictionContext.userRole, 'employees', 'view'),
  );
  const params: unknown[] = [getAgencyId()];
  const where = saleWhere(filters, params);
  return database.withTenantTransaction(async (client) => {
    const entries = await client.query<{ id: string; amount: string; status: string }>(
      `SELECT ce.id, ce.amount::text AS amount, ce.status
         FROM commission_entries ce JOIN sales s ON s.agency_id = ce.agency_id AND s.id = ce.sale_id
        WHERE ${where} AND ce.agency_id = $1
        ORDER BY ce.created_at, ce.id LIMIT ${COMMISSION_PROJECTION_LIMIT + 1}`,
      params,
    );
    if (entries.rows.length > COMMISSION_PROJECTION_LIMIT) throw new ValidationError('Too many rows for this report; narrow the filters');
    const rows: CommissionRow[] = [];
    for (const entry of entries.rows) {
      const release = await computeEntryRelease(client, getAgencyId(), entry.id);
      rows.push({ entryId: entry.id, amount: Number(entry.amount), released: release.released, paid: release.paid, status: entry.status });
    }
    return projectCommissions(rows);
  });

673: async function getAllocatedReceivableAmount(

/**
 * Sales import planner (IMP-01) -- pure: no I/O. Turns parsed rows into a plan
 * (dry run) that the service later executes. Rules:
 *  - natural key = `<source system>|<external_ref>`: a sale is never created twice;
 *  - A2: category/supplier/destination missing or unknown do NOT reject; the sale
 *    is imported and reported as "a completar";
 *  - the customer is linked by CPF and must already exist (never created silently);
 *  - an import never creates commissions or receivables (historical sales only).
 */
import { createHash } from 'node:crypto';

export const MAX_IMPORT_ROWS = 2000;

export const IMPORT_COLUMNS = [
  'external_ref', 'sale_date', 'customer_cpf', 'category', 'supplier', 'total', 'discount',
  'origin', 'destination', 'travel_start_date', 'notes',
] as const;

export type SalesImportRow = Partial<Record<(typeof IMPORT_COLUMNS)[number], string>>;

export interface SalesImportLookups {
  /** digits-only CPF -> customer id */
  customersByCpf: ReadonlyMap<string, string>;
  /** normalized name -> category id */
  categoriesByName: ReadonlyMap<string, string>;
  suppliersByName: ReadonlyMap<string, string>;
  /** natural keys that already exist (previous imports) */
  existingKeys: ReadonlySet<string>;
  /** civil date, to refuse future sale dates */
  today: string;
}

export interface PlannedRow {
  row: number;
  status: 'NEW' | 'ALREADY_IMPORTED' | 'DUPLICATE_IN_FILE' | 'REJECTED';
  naturalKey: string;
  customerId: string | null;
  categoryId: string | null;
  supplierId: string | null;
  saleDate: string | null;
  total: number;
  discount: number;
  origin: string | null;
  destination: string | null;
  travelStartDate: string | null;
  notes: string | null;
  toComplete: Array<'category' | 'supplier' | 'destination'>;
  errors: Array<{ column: string; message: string }>;
}

export interface SalesImportPlan {
  sourceSystem: string;
  rows: PlannedRow[];
  summary: { total: number; new: number; alreadyImported: number; duplicateInFile: number; rejected: number; toComplete: number };
}

export const normalizeName = (value: string | undefined): string =>
  (value ?? '').normalize('NFD').replace(/[�?-��]/g, '').trim().toLowerCase();

function validCpf(digits: string): boolean {
  if (digits.length !== 11 || /^(\d)\1{10}$/.test(digits)) return false;
  const check = (length: number) => {
    let sum = 0;
    for (let i = 0; i < length; i++) sum += Number(digits[i]) * (length + 1 - i);
    const rest = (sum * 10) % 11;
    return rest === 10 ? 0 : rest;
  };
  return check(9) === Number(digits[9]) && check(10) === Number(digits[10]);
}

export function normalizeCpf(value: string | undefined): string | null {
  const digits = (value ?? '').replace(/\D/g, '');
  return digits.length === 11 ? digits : null;
}

/** sales.amount/total are NUMERIC(10,2). */
const MAX_MONEY = 99_999_999.99;

function parseMoney(value: string | undefined): number | null {
  if (value === undefined) return null;
  // Strict: "1.234,56", "1234,56", "1234.56" or "1234", optional "R$" prefix. Anything else is rejected, never guessed.
  let text = value.trim().replace(/^R\$\s*/, '');
  if (!/^-?(\d{1,3}(\.\d{3})+|\d+)(,\d{1,4})?$|^-?\d+\.\d{1,4}$/.test(text)) return null;
  if (text.includes(',')) text = text.replace(/\./g, '').replace(',', '.');
  else if (/^-?\d{1,3}(\.\d{3})+$/.test(text)) text = text.replace(/\./g, '');
  const number = Number(text);
  if (!Number.isFinite(number) || Math.abs(number) > MAX_MONEY) return null;
  // half-up to cents, decimal safe enough for BRL magnitudes
  return Math.round((number + Number.EPSILON) * 100 + (number >= 0 ? 1e-9 : -1e-9)) / 100;
}

const isCivilDate = (value: string | undefined): value is string => {
  if (!value || !/^\d{4}-\d{2}-\d{2}$/.test(value)) return false;
  const date = new Date(`${value}T00:00:00.000Z`);
  return !Number.isNaN(date.getTime()) && date.toISOString().slice(0, 10) === value;
};

export function planSalesImport(
  rows: readonly SalesImportRow[],
  lookups: SalesImportLookups,
  sourceSystem: string,
): SalesImportPlan {
  if (rows.length > MAX_IMPORT_ROWS) throw new RangeError(`A importa��ǜo aceita no mǭximo ${MAX_IMPORT_ROWS} linhas`);
  const allowed = new Set<string>(IMPORT_COLUMNS);
  for (const row of rows) {
    for (const column of Object.keys(row)) if (!allowed.has(column)) throw new RangeError(`Unknown column "${column}"`);
  }
  const seen = new Set<string>();
  const planned: PlannedRow[] = rows.map((raw, index) => {
    const errors: PlannedRow['errors'] = [];
    const ref = (raw.external_ref ?? '').trim();
    if (ref === '' || ref.length > 100 || ref.includes('|')) {
      errors.push({ column: 'external_ref', message: 'Informe o nǧmero da venda de origem (atǸ 100 caracteres, sem "|")' });
    }
    const naturalKey = `${sourceSystem}|${ref}`;

    const saleDate = raw.sale_date?.trim();
    if (!isCivilDate(saleDate)) errors.push({ column: 'sale_date', message: 'Data da venda invǭlida (use AAAA-MM-DD)' });
    else if (saleDate > lookups.today) errors.push({ column: 'sale_date', message: 'A data da venda nǜo pode ser futura' });

    const cpf = normalizeCpf(raw.customer_cpf);
    let customerId: string | null = null;
    if (!cpf || !validCpf(cpf)) errors.push({ column: 'customer_cpf', message: 'CPF do cliente invǭlido' });
    else {
      customerId = lookups.customersByCpf.get(cpf) ?? null;
      if (!customerId) errors.push({ column: 'customer_cpf', message: 'Cliente nǜo encontrado para este CPF (cadastre antes de importar)' });
    }

    const total = parseMoney(raw.total);
    if (total === null || total <= 0) errors.push({ column: 'total', message: 'Valor total invǭlido' });
    const discountText = raw.discount?.trim();
    const discount = discountText ? parseMoney(discountText) : 0;
    if (discount === null || discount < 0) errors.push({ column: 'discount', message: 'Desconto invǭlido' });
    else if (total !== null && total > 0 && discount > total) errors.push({ column: 'discount', message: 'O desconto nǜo pode exceder o total' });

    const travel = raw.travel_start_date?.trim();
    if (travel && !isCivilDate(travel)) errors.push({ column: 'travel_start_date', message: 'Data de embarque invǭlida' });

    const category = normalizeName(raw.category);
    const supplier = normalizeName(raw.supplier);
    const categoryId = category ? (lookups.categoriesByName.get(category) ?? null) : null;
    const supplierId = supplier ? (lookups.suppliersByName.get(supplier) ?? null) : null;
    const destination = raw.destination?.trim() || null;
    const toComplete: PlannedRow['toComplete'] = [];
    if (!categoryId) toComplete.push('category');
    if (!supplierId) toComplete.push('supplier');
    if (!destination) toComplete.push('destination');

    let status: PlannedRow['status'] = 'NEW';
    if (errors.length > 0) status = 'REJECTED';
    else if (lookups.existingKeys.has(naturalKey)) status = 'ALREADY_IMPORTED';
    else if (seen.has(naturalKey)) status = 'DUPLICATE_IN_FILE';
    if (status === 'NEW') seen.add(naturalKey);

    return {
      row: index + 1,
      status,
      naturalKey,
      customerId,
      categoryId,
      supplierId,
      saleDate: isCivilDate(saleDate) ? saleDate : null,
      total: total ?? 0,
      discount: discount ?? 0,
      origin: raw.origin?.trim() || null,
      destination,
      travelStartDate: travel && isCivilDate(travel) ? travel : null,
      notes: raw.notes?.trim() || null,
      toComplete: status === 'NEW' ? toComplete : [],
      errors,
    };
  });
  const count = (status: PlannedRow['status']) => planned.filter((row) => row.status === status).length;
  return {
    sourceSystem,
    rows: planned,
    summary: {
      total: planned.length,
      new: count('NEW'),
      alreadyImported: count('ALREADY_IMPORTED'),
/**
 * Sales import service (IMP-01): dry run, then an atomic, idempotent confirm.
 * The plan is computed by the pure planner; this file only loads the lookups
 * of the session's agency and writes. Imported sales are historical: this code
 * never touches commissions or receivables (a test proves the counts do not move).
 */
import { getAgencyId, getUserId, requireRole } from '../../../packages/domain/tenant-context';
import { UserRole } from '../../../packages/domain/types';
import { AuditEventType, recordAuditEvent } from './audit-log';
import type { DatabaseRuntime, TenantTransactionClient } from './database';
import { ConflictError, ValidationError } from './errors';
import {
  contentHash,
  IMPORT_COLUMNS,
  MAX_IMPORT_ROWS,
  normalizeName,
  planSalesImport,
  type SalesImportPlan,
  type SalesImportRow,
} from './sales-import-plan';

export interface SalesImportInput {
  sourceSystem: string;
  rows: SalesImportRow[];
  /** Only on confirm: the hash the user saw in the dry run. */
  contentHash?: string;
}

export function parseSalesImportInput(body: unknown, confirm: boolean): SalesImportInput {
  if (!body || typeof body !== 'object' || Array.isArray(body)) throw new ValidationError('Request body must be an object');
  const record = body as Record<string, unknown>;
  const allowed = confirm ? ['sourceSystem', 'rows', 'contentHash'] : ['sourceSystem', 'rows'];
  if (Object.keys(record).some((key) => !allowed.includes(key))) throw new ValidationError('Unknown field in request body');
  const sourceSystem = typeof record.sourceSystem === 'string' ? record.sourceSystem.trim() : '';
  if (sourceSystem.length < 1 || sourceSystem.length > 60 || sourceSystem.includes('|')) {
    throw new ValidationError('Field "sourceSystem" is required (1 to 60 characters, without "|")');
  }
  if (!Array.isArray(record.rows)) throw new ValidationError('Field "rows" must be a list');
  if (record.rows.length === 0 || record.rows.length > MAX_IMPORT_ROWS) {
    throw new ValidationError(`Field "rows" must have 1 to ${MAX_IMPORT_ROWS} rows`);
  }
  const columns = new Set<string>(IMPORT_COLUMNS);
  const rows = record.rows.map((raw: unknown) => {
    if (!raw || typeof raw !== 'object' || Array.isArray(raw)) throw new ValidationError('Each row must be an object');
    const out: Record<string, string> = {};
    for (const [key, value] of Object.entries(raw as Record<string, unknown>)) {
      if (!columns.has(key)) throw new ValidationError(`Unknown column "${key}"`);
      if (typeof value !== 'string' || value.length > 500) throw new ValidationError(`Column "${key}" must be text (max 500)`);
      out[key] = value;
    }
    return out;
  });
  if (confirm && (typeof record.contentHash !== 'string' || !/^[0-9a-f]{64}$/.test(record.contentHash))) {
    throw new ValidationError('Field "contentHash" (from the dry run) is required');
  }
  return { sourceSystem, rows, ...(confirm ? { contentHash: record.contentHash as string } : {}) };
}

async function buildPlan(client: TenantTransactionClient, input: SalesImportInput): Promise<SalesImportPlan> {
  const agencyId = getAgencyId();
  const customers = await client.query<{ id: string; cpf: string }>(
    `SELECT id, regexp_replace(cpf, '\\D', '', 'g') AS cpf FROM customers WHERE agency_id = $1 AND cpf IS NOT NULL AND deleted_at IS NULL`,
    [agencyId],
  );
  const categories = await client.query<{ id: string; name: string }>(
    `SELECT id, name FROM commission_categories WHERE agency_id = $1`,
    [agencyId],
  );
  const suppliers = await client.query<{ id: string; name: string }>(`SELECT id, name FROM suppliers WHERE agency_id = $1 AND active`, [agencyId]);
  const keys = await client.query<{ source_system: string; external_ref: string }>(
    `SELECT source_system, external_ref FROM sale_import_keys WHERE agency_id = $1 AND source_system = $2`,
    [agencyId, input.sourceSystem],
  );
  const byName = (rows: Array<{ id: string; name: string }>) => {
    // An ambiguous name (two rows with the same normalized name) is not matched: it is reported "a completar".
    const map = new Map<string, string | null>();
    for (const row of rows) {
      const key = normalizeName(row.name);
      map.set(key, map.has(key) ? null : row.id);
    }
    return new Map([...map].filter((entry): entry is [string, string] => entry[1] !== null));
  };
  const cpfs = new Map<string, string | null>();
  for (const row of customers.rows) cpfs.set(row.cpf, cpfs.has(row.cpf) ? null : row.id);
  return planSalesImport(
    input.rows,
    {
      customersByCpf: new Map([...cpfs].filter((entry): entry is [string, string] => entry[1] !== null)),
      categoriesByName: byName(categories.rows),
      suppliersByName: byName(suppliers.rows),
      existingKeys: new Set(keys.rows.map((row) => `${row.source_system}|${row.external_ref}`)),
      today: new Intl.DateTimeFormat('en-CA', { timeZone: 'America/Sao_Paulo' }).format(new Date()),
    },
    input.sourceSystem,
  );
}

export async function dryRunSalesImport(database: DatabaseRuntime, input: SalesImportInput) {
  requireRole(UserRole.MANAGER);
  const hash = contentHash(input.rows, input.sourceSystem);
  return database.withTenantTransaction(async (client) => {
    const plan = await buildPlan(client, input);
    const previous = await client.query<{ summary: unknown; created_at: Date }>(
      `SELECT summary, created_at FROM sale_import_batches WHERE agency_id = $1 AND content_hash = $2`,
      [getAgencyId(), hash],
    );
    return { contentHash: hash, alreadyImported: previous.rows[0] !== undefined, plan };
  });
}

export async function confirmSalesImport(database: DatabaseRuntime, input: SalesImportInput) {
  requireRole(UserRole.MANAGER);
  const hash = contentHash(input.rows, input.sourceSystem);
  if (input.contentHash !== hash) throw new ConflictError('The file differs from the one validated in the dry run');
  const agencyId = getAgencyId();
  const userId = getUserId();
  return database.withTenantTransaction(async (client) => {
    // One import per agency and source system at a time: the loser of a race replays the stored result or
    // re-plans against the keys the winner just wrote (different files may share natural keys).
    await client.query('SELECT pg_advisory_xact_lock(hashtextextended($1, 0))', [`sales-import:${agencyId}:${input.sourceSystem}`]);
    const replay = await client.query<{ summary: unknown }>(
      `SELECT summary FROM sale_import_batches WHERE agency_id = $1 AND content_hash = $2`,
      [agencyId, hash],
    );
    if (replay.rows[0]) return { replayed: true, contentHash: hash, summary: replay.rows[0].summary };

    const plan = await buildPlan(client, input);
    // Batches are append-only (no UPDATE grant): the summary is known from the plan before anything is written.
    const summary = {
      ...plan.summary,
      created: plan.summary.new,
      rejections: plan.rows.filter((r) => r.status === 'REJECTED').map((r) => ({ row: r.row, errors: r.errors })),
      toCompleteRows: plan.rows.filter((r) => r.toComplete.length > 0).map((r) => ({ row: r.row, reasons: r.toComplete })),
    };
    const batch = await client.query<{ id: string }>(
      `INSERT INTO sale_import_batches (agency_id, source_system, content_hash, summary, created_by_user_id)
       VALUES ($1, $2, $3, $4::jsonb, $5) RETURNING id`,
      [agencyId, input.sourceSystem, hash, JSON.stringify(summary), userId],
    );
    const batchId = batch.rows[0]!.id;
    let created = 0;
    for (const row of plan.rows) {
      if (row.status !== 'NEW') continue;
      const sale = await client.query<{ id: string }>(
        `INSERT INTO sales (agency_id, customer_id, user_id, amount, discount, total, status, sale_date, category_id,
                            origin_text, destination_text, travel_start_date, notes)
         VALUES ($1, $2, $3, $4, $5, $6, 'CONFIRMED', $7::date, $8, $9, $10, $11::date, $12) RETURNING id`,
        [agencyId, row.customerId, userId, row.total, row.discount, Math.round((row.total - row.discount) * 100) / 100,
          row.saleDate, row.categoryId, row.origin, row.destination, row.travelStartDate,
          [row.notes, `Importada de ${input.sourceSystem} #${row.naturalKey.split('|')[1]}`].filter(Boolean).join(' | ')],
      );
      const saleId = sale.rows[0]!.id;
      if (row.supplierId) {
        await client.query(
          `INSERT INTO sale_items (agency_id, sale_id, description, supplier_id, qty, unit_price, discount, line_total, line_margin, created_by_user_id)
           VALUES ($1, $2, $3, $4, 1, $5, $6, $7, 0, $8)`,
          [agencyId, saleId, `Importado de ${input.sourceSystem}`, row.supplierId, row.total, row.discount,
            Math.round((row.total - row.discount) * 100) / 100, userId],
        );
      }
      await client.query(
        `INSERT INTO sale_import_keys (agency_id, source_system, external_ref, sale_id, batch_id) VALUES ($1, $2, $3, $4, $5)`,
        [agencyId, input.sourceSystem, row.naturalKey.split('|')[1], saleId, batchId],
      );
      created += 1;
    }
    await recordAuditEvent(client, {
      eventType: AuditEventType.SALES_IMPORTED,
      entityType: 'sale_import_batch',
      entityId: batchId,
      required: true,
      metadata: { result: `${created} created, ${plan.summary.rejected} rejected, ${plan.summary.alreadyImported + plan.summary.duplicateInFile} skipped`, reasonCode: `hash:${hash.slice(0, 16)}` },
    });
    return { replayed: false, contentHash: hash, summary };
  });
}

/**
 * Versioned / progressive employee commission rules and the canonical accrual (Onda E1).
 * Tenant comes from the session context only. Versions are append-only (migration 107);
 * the in-force version is projected by effective_from / version_number, never stored as valid_until.
 */

import { getAgencyId, getUserId } from '../../../packages/domain/tenant-context';
import type { DatabaseRuntime, TenantTransactionClient } from './database';
import { AuditEventType, recordAuditEvent } from './audit-log';
import { ConflictError, NotFoundError, ValidationError } from './errors';
import { getSaleMargin } from './financial';
import {
  calculateCommission,
  selectEffectiveVersion,
  type CommissionRuleVersion,
  type CommissionTier,
} from './commission-calculation';

const AGENCY_TZ_TODAY = `(now() AT TIME ZONE 'America/Sao_Paulo')::date::text`;

export interface RuleVersionInput {
  effectiveFrom: string;
  mode: 'PERCENTAGE' | 'FIXED' | 'NONE';
  calculationMetric: 'GROSS' | 'MARGIN';
  value?: number | null;
  applyOn?: 'TOTAL' | 'EXCESS';
  excessThreshold?: number | null;
  progressive?: boolean;
  tiers?: Array<{ fromAmount: number; toAmount: number | null; rate: number }>;
  reason?: string;
}

interface VersionRow {
  id: string;
  scope_id: string;
  version_number: number;
  effective_from: string;
  mode: 'PERCENTAGE' | 'FIXED' | 'NONE';
  calculation_metric: 'GROSS' | 'MARGIN';
  value: string | null;
  apply_on: 'TOTAL' | 'EXCESS';
  excess_threshold: string | null;
  progressive: boolean;
  reason: string | null;
}

const VERSION_COLUMNS = `id, scope_id, version_number, effective_from::text AS effective_from, mode, calculation_metric,
  value::text AS value, apply_on, excess_threshold::text AS excess_threshold, progressive, reason`;

function toRuleVersion(row: VersionRow, tiers: CommissionTier[]): CommissionRuleVersion {
  return {
    scope_id: row.scope_id,
    version_id: row.id,
    version_number: row.version_number,
    effective_from: row.effective_from,
    mode: row.mode,
    calculation_base: row.calculation_metric,
    value: row.value === null ? null : Number(row.value),
    apply_on: row.apply_on,
    excess_threshold: row.excess_threshold === null ? null : Number(row.excess_threshold),
    progressive: row.progressive,
    tiers,
  };
}

async function loadTiers(client: TenantTransactionClient, agencyId: string, versionId: string): Promise<CommissionTier[]> {
  const result = await client.query<{ tier_number: number; from_amount: string; to_amount: string | null; rate: string }>(
    `SELECT tier_number, from_amount::text, to_amount::text, rate::text FROM employee_commission_rule_tiers
      WHERE agency_id = $1 AND version_id = $2 ORDER BY tier_number`,
    [agencyId, versionId],
  );
  return result.rows.map((t) => ({
    tier_number: t.tier_number,
    from_amount: Number(t.from_amount),
    to_amount: t.to_amount === null ? null : Number(t.to_amount),
    rate: Number(t.rate),
  }));
}

function assertDate(value: string, field: string): void {
  if (!/^\d{4}-\d{2}-\d{2}$/.test(value) || new Date(`${value}T00:00:00Z`).toISOString().slice(0, 10) !== value) {
    throw new ValidationError(`Field "${field}" must be a valid date (YYYY-MM-DD)`);
  }
}

async function insertVersion(
  client: TenantTransactionClient,
  agencyId: string,
  scopeId: string,
  input: RuleVersionInput,
): Promise<string> {
  assertDate(input.effectiveFrom, 'effectiveFrom');
  const today = (await client.query<{ d: string }>(`SELECT ${AGENCY_TZ_TODAY} AS d`)).rows[0]!.d;
  if (input.effectiveFrom < today) throw new ValidationError('A rule revision cannot take effect in the past');
  const progressive = input.progressive === true;
  const tiers = input.tiers ?? [];
  if (progressive) {
    // fail closed on a malformed ladder before touching the database
    try {
    calculateCommission(
      {
        scope_id: scopeId, version_id: 'check', version_number: 1, effective_from: input.effectiveFrom, mode: 'PERCENTAGE',
        calculation_base: input.calculationMetric, value: null, apply_on: 'TOTAL', excess_threshold: null, progressive: true,
        tiers: tiers.map((t, i) => ({ tier_number: i + 1, from_amount: t.fromAmount, to_amount: t.toAmount, rate: t.rate })),
      },
      { gross_amount: 0, margin_amount: 0 },
      0,
    );
    } catch (error) {
      if (error instanceof RangeError) throw new ValidationError(error.message);
      throw error;
    }
  } else if (tiers.length > 0) {
    throw new ValidationError('Tiers are only allowed on a progressive version');
  }
  const next = await client.query<{ n: number }>(
    `SELECT COALESCE(max(version_number), 0) + 1 AS n FROM employee_commission_rule_versions WHERE agency_id = $1 AND scope_id = $2`,
    [agencyId, scopeId],
  );
  const inserted = await client.query<{ id: string }>(
    `INSERT INTO employee_commission_rule_versions
       (agency_id, scope_id, version_number, effective_from, revised_by, reason, mode, calculation_metric, value,
        apply_on, excess_threshold, progressive)
     VALUES ($1, $2, $3, $4::date, $5, $6, $7, $8, $9, $10, $11, $12) RETURNING id`,
    [agencyId, scopeId, next.rows[0]!.n, input.effectiveFrom, getUserId(), input.reason ?? null, input.mode,
      input.calculationMetric, input.value ?? null, input.applyOn ?? 'TOTAL', input.excessThreshold ?? null, progressive],
  );
  const versionId = inserted.rows[0]!.id;
  for (const [index, tier] of tiers.entries()) {
    await client.query(
      `INSERT INTO employee_commission_rule_tiers (agency_id, version_id, tier_number, from_amount, to_amount, rate) VALUES ($1, $2, $3, $4, $5, $6)`,
      [agencyId, versionId, index + 1, tier.fromAmount, tier.toAmount, tier.rate],
    );
  }
  return versionId;
}

export async function createRuleScope(
// PermissionRestriction (Agent 01 SaaS Admin).
//
// Additive-only, tenant-scoped narrowing of what a role may do WITHIN
// THAT TENANT. This is layered ON TOP OF requireRole() -- never a
// replacement for it -- and can only take permissions away, never grant
// permissions the base RBAC hierarchy (OWNER>ADMIN>MANAGER>AGENT>VIEWER)
// would not already allow. See governance/AUTONOMY.md and
// security/NON_NEGOTIABLES.md: RBAC base meanings must never change.
//
// Guardrails enforced here (not just at the DB layer):
//   * OWNER and ADMIN can never be restricted -- prevents a tenant from
//     locking itself out of its own agency (self-lockout). Enforced by
//     both the CHECK constraint on permission_restrictions.role and the
//     assertion in createPermissionRestriction() below.
//   * assertNotRestricted() is called AFTER requireRole() at each route
//     boundary that opts in -- it can only reject a request that RBAC
//     already would have allowed; it can never let through a request
//     RBAC denies (there is no bypass path here at all).
import { getAgencyId, getUserId, requireRole } from '../../../packages/domain/tenant-context';
import { UserRole } from '../../../packages/domain/types';
import type { DatabaseRuntime, TenantTransactionClient } from './database';
import { ForbiddenError } from '../../../packages/domain/tenant-context';
import { ConflictError, ValidationError } from './errors';
import { AuditEventType, recordAuditEvent } from './audit-log';

export interface PermissionRestriction {
  id: string;
  agencyId: string;
  role: UserRole;
  resource: string;
  action: string;
  createdByUserId: string;
  createdAt: Date;
}

interface PermissionRestrictionRow {
  id: string;
  agency_id: string;
  role: UserRole;
  resource: string;
  action: string;
  created_by_user_id: string;
  created_at: string;
}

// Roles a tenant is allowed to restrict. OWNER/ADMIN are intentionally
// excluded -- restricting them would let a tenant lock itself out of its
// own agency, which this mechanism must never permit.
const RESTRICTABLE_ROLES: ReadonlySet<UserRole> = new Set([
  UserRole.MANAGER,
  UserRole.AGENT,
  UserRole.VIEWER,
]);

function toPermissionRestriction(row: PermissionRestrictionRow): PermissionRestriction {
  return {
    id: row.id,
    agencyId: row.agency_id,
    role: row.role,
    resource: row.resource,
    action: row.action,
    createdByUserId: row.created_by_user_id,
    createdAt: new Date(row.created_at),
  };
}

export interface CreatePermissionRestrictionInput {
  role: UserRole;
  resource: string;
  action: string;
}

export async function createPermissionRestriction(
  database: DatabaseRuntime,
  input: CreatePermissionRestrictionInput,
): Promise<PermissionRestriction> {
  // Only OWNER/ADMIN may configure restrictions for their tenant.
  requireRole(UserRole.ADMIN);
  const agencyId = getAgencyId();
  const createdByUserId = getUserId();

  if (!RESTRICTABLE_ROLES.has(input.role)) {
    throw new ValidationError(
      'Apenas os papǸis MANAGER, AGENT e VIEWER podem ser restringidos. OWNER e ADMIN nunca podem ser restringidos (previne bloqueio do pr��prio tenant).',
    );
  }
  const resource = input.resource?.trim();
  const action = input.action?.trim();
  if (!resource || !action) {
    throw new ValidationError('resource e action sǜo obrigat��rios');
  }

  return database.withTenantTransaction(async (client) => {
    let result;
    try {
      result = await client.query<PermissionRestrictionRow>(
        `INSERT INTO permission_restrictions (agency_id, role, resource, action, created_by_user_id)
         VALUES ($1, $2, $3, $4, $5)
         RETURNING id, agency_id, role, resource, action, created_by_user_id, created_at`,
        [agencyId, input.role, resource, action, createdByUserId],
      );
    } catch (error: unknown) {
      if (isUniqueViolation(error)) {
        throw new ConflictError('Esta restri��ǜo jǭ existe para este papel/recurso/a��ǜo');
      }
      throw error;
    }
    const row = result.rows[0];
    if (!row) {
      throw new Error('Permission restriction insert did not return a row');

770:   quantity: number | undefined;
771:   calculationBase: number;
772:   rate: number | undefined;
773:   amount: number;
774:   status: CommissionEntryStatus;
775:   approvedAt: Date | undefined;
776:   approvedBy: string | undefined;
777:   paidAt: Date | undefined;
778:   notes: string | undefined;
779:   createdAt: Date;
780:   updatedAt: Date;
  return [...suppliers.values()]
    .sort((a, b) => String(a.id ?? '').localeCompare(String(b.id ?? '')))
    .map((supplier) => ({
      supplierId: supplier.id,
      expected: money(supplier.expected),
      received: money(supplier.received),
      difference: money(supplier.expected - supplier.received),
    }));
}

// ---------------------------------------------------------- employee commission

export interface CommissionRow {
  entryId: string;
  amount: number;
  /** Amount released for payment by the customer receipts (release policy). */
  released: number;
  paid: number;
  status: string;
}

export interface CommissionProjection {
  forecast: number;
  released: number;
  paid: number;
  releasedNotPaid: number;
  notYetReleased: number;
}

export function projectCommissions(rows: readonly CommissionRow[]): CommissionProjection {
  let forecast = 0;
  let released = 0;
  let paid = 0;
  for (const row of rows) {
    if (row.status === 'CANCELLED') continue;
    const amount = cents(row.amount);
    const rel = Math.min(Math.max(cents(row.released), 0), amount);
    const pd = Math.min(Math.max(cents(row.paid), 0), rel);
    forecast += amount;
    released += rel;
    paid += pd;
  }
  return {
    forecast: money(forecast),
    released: money(released),
    paid: money(paid),
    releasedNotPaid: money(released - paid),
    notYetReleased: money(forecast - released),
  };
}
